Here is the comprehensive, production-grade upgrade for your custom Flink 2.3 lifecycle operator.

This update builds directly into the reconciliation loop to automate proactive maintenance and infrastructure exposure:

PreStop Lifecycle Hooks: Configured within the JobManager deployment to automatically execute a clean checkpoint/savepoint trigger to a durable storage location (/opt/flink/data/savepoints) prior to an upgrade or scaling event.
OpenShift Route Generation: Automatically maps and rolls out an authenticated OpenShift network Route for each cluster to securely expose the *-jobmanager-ui dashboard.


🐹 1. Complete Production Controller (controller.go)

package main

import (
    "context"
    "fmt"
    "strconv"

    routev1 "://github.com"
    appsv1 "k8s.io/api/apps/v1"
    corev1 "k8s.io/api/core/v1"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/errors"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/resource"
    metav1 "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/runtime"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/util/intstr"
    ctrl "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/client"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/log"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/predicate"
)

type FlinkClusterReconciler struct {
    client.Client
    Scheme *runtime.Scheme
}

func (r *FlinkClusterReconciler) Reconcile(ctx context.Context, req ctrl.Request) (ctrl.Result, error) {
    logger := log.FromContext(ctx)

    var configMap corev1.ConfigMap
    if err := r.Get(ctx, req.NamespacedName, &configMap); err != nil {
        if errors.IsNotFound(err) {
            logger.Info("Trigger ConfigMap deleted. Cascading deletion is handled via OwnerReferences.")
            return ctrl.Result{}, nil
        }
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    clusterMode := configMap.Data["cluster-mode"]
    image := configMap.Data["flink-image"]
    if image == "" {
        image = "flink:2.3"
    }
    parallelismStr := configMap.Data["parallelism"]
    parallelism, err := strconv.Atoi(parallelismStr)
    if err != nil || parallelism <= 0 {
        parallelism = 2
    }

    appJarPath := configMap.Data["app-jar-path"]
    entryClass := configMap.Data["job-entry-class"]
    savepointPath := configMap.Data["savepoint-path"]

    // Reconcile Networking Layer Services
    jmServiceName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-jobmanager-rpc", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileService(ctx, req.Namespace, jmServiceName, configMap.Name, 6123, "rpc", &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    jmUIServiceName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-jobmanager-ui", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileService(ctx, req.Namespace, jmUIServiceName, configMap.Name, 8081, "ui", &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    // FEATURE 2: Automatically generate and maintain OpenShift Route for UI dashboard exposure
    if err := r.reconcileOpenShiftRoute(ctx, req.Namespace, configMap.Name, jmUIServiceName, &configMap); err != nil {
        logger.Error(err, "Failed to reconcile OpenShift Route asset", "Cluster", configMap.Name)
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    // Reconcile Infrastructure Deployments (with Feature 1 inside)
    jmDeploymentName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-jobmanager", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileJobManager(ctx, req.Namespace, jmDeploymentName, clusterMode, image, appJarPath, entryClass, savepointPath, &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    tmStatefulSetName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-taskmanager", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileTaskManagers(ctx, req.Namespace, tmStatefulSetName, image, jmServiceName, int32(parallelism), &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    return ctrl.Result{}, nil
}

func (r *FlinkClusterReconciler) reconcileService(ctx context.Context, ns, name, clusterName string, port int32, portName string, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
    foundSVC := &corev1.Service{}
    err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: name}, foundSVC)
    if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
        svc := &corev1.Service{
            ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
                Name:      name,
                Namespace: ns,
                OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{
                    *metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind()),
                },
            },
            Spec: corev1.ServiceSpec{
                Selector: map[string]string{"flink-role": "jobmanager", "flink-cluster": clusterName},
                Ports: []corev1.ServicePort{
                    {Port: port, Name: portName, TargetPort: intstr.FromInt32(port)},
                },
            },
        }
        return r.Create(ctx, svc)
    }
    return err
}

// FEATURE 2 Implementation: Programmatic OpenShift Route Reconciliation Loop
func (r *FlinkClusterReconciler) reconcileOpenShiftRoute(ctx context.Context, ns, clusterName, svcName string, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
    routeName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-ui-route", clusterName)
    foundRoute := &routev1.Route{}
    err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: routeName}, foundRoute)

    desiredRoute := &routev1.Route{
        ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
            Name:      routeName,
            Namespace: ns,
            OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{
                *metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind()),
            },
        },
        Spec: routev1.RouteSpec{
            To: routev1.RouteTargetReference{
                Kind: "Service",
                Name: svcName,
            },
            Port: &routev1.RoutePort{
                TargetPort: intstr.FromString("ui"),
            },
            TLS: &routev1.TLSConfig{
                Termination:                   routev1.TLSTerminationEdge,
                InsecureEdgeTerminationPolicy: routev1.InsecureEdgeTerminationPolicyRedirect,
            },
        },
    }

    if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
        return r.Create(ctx, desiredRoute)
    }
    return err
}

func (r *FlinkClusterReconciler) reconcileJobManager(ctx context.Context, ns, name, mode, image, jarPath, entryClass, savepoint string, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
    foundJM := &appsv1.Deployment{}
    err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: name}, foundJM)

    var commandArgs []string
    if mode == "application" {
        commandArgs = []string{"standalone-job", fmt.Sprintf("--job-classname=%s", entryClass)}
        if savepoint != "" {
            commandArgs = append(commandArgs, fmt.Sprintf("--fromSavepoint=%s", savepoint))
        }
        if jarPath != "" {
            commandArgs = append(commandArgs, jarPath)
        }
    } else {
        commandArgs = []string{"jobmanager"}
    }

    replicas := int32(1)
    desiredDeployment := &appsv1.Deployment{
        ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
            Name:      name,
            Namespace: ns,
            OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{
                *metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind()),
            },
        },
        Spec: appsv1.DeploymentSpec{
            Replicas: &replicas,
            Selector: &metav1.LabelSelector{MatchLabels: map[string]string{"flink-role": "jobmanager", "flink-cluster": owner.Name}},
            Template: corev1.PodTemplateSpec{
                Metadata: metav1.ObjectMeta{Labels: map[string]string{"flink-role": "jobmanager", "flink-cluster": owner.Name}},
                Spec: corev1.PodSpec{
                    Containers: []corev1.Container{{
                        Name:  "jobmanager",
                        Image: image,
                        Args:  commandArgs,
                        Ports: []corev1.ContainerPort{
                            {ContainerPort: 6123, Name: "rpc"},
                            {ContainerPort: 8081, Name: "ui"},
                        },
                        Env: []corev1.EnvVar{
                            {Name: "JOB_MANAGER_RPC_ADDRESS", Value: fmt.Sprintf("%s-jobmanager-rpc", owner.Name)},
                        },
                        // FEATURE 1 Implementation: Proactive Maintenance PreStop Lifecycle Checkpoint Handler
                        Lifecycle: &corev1.Lifecycle{
                            PreStop: &corev1.LifecycleHandler{
                                Exec: &corev1.ExecAction{
                                    Command: []string{
                                        "/bin/sh", "-c",
                                        "flink stop --stopWithSavepoint --savepointPath /opt/flink/data/savepoints $(flink list -r | grep RUNNING | awk '{print $4}') || true",
                                    },
                                },
                            },
                        },
                        VolumeMounts: []corev1.VolumeMount{{
                            Name:      "flink-shared-storage",
                            MountPath: "/opt/flink/data",
                        }},
                    }},
                    Volumes: []corev1.Volume{{
                        Name: "flink-shared-storage",
                        VolumeSource: corev1.VolumeSource{
                            EmptyDir: &corev1.EmptyDirVolumeSource{}, // Interchangeable with persistent networks/S3 mount pathways
                        },
                    }},
                },
            },
        },
    }

    if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
        return r.Create(ctx, desiredDeployment)
    } else if err == nil {
        if foundJM.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Image != image {
            foundJM.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Image = image
            foundJM.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Args = commandArgs
            return r.Update(ctx, foundJM)
        }
    }
    return err
}

func (r *FlinkClusterReconciler) reconcileTaskManagers(ctx context.Context, ns, name, image, jmSvcName string, replicas int32, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
    foundSTS := &appsv1.StatefulSet{}
    err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: name}, foundSTS)

    desiredSTS := &appsv1.StatefulSet{
        ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
            Name:      name,
            Namespace: ns,
            OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{
                *metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind()),
            },
        },
        Spec: appsv1.StatefulSetSpec{
            Replicas:            &replicas,
            ServiceName:         jmSvcName,
            PodManagementPolicy: appsv1.ParallelPodManagement,
            Selector: &metav1.LabelSelector{
                MatchLabels: map[string]string{"flink-role": "taskmanager", "flink-cluster": owner.Name},
            },
            Template: corev1.PodTemplateSpec{
                Metadata: metav1.ObjectMeta{Labels: map[string]string{"flink-role": "taskmanager", "flink-cluster": owner.Name}},
                Spec: corev1.PodSpec{
                    Containers: []corev1.Container{{
                        Name:  "taskmanager",
                        Image: image,
                        Args:  []string{"taskmanager"},
                        Env: []corev1.EnvVar{
                            {Name: "JOB_MANAGER_RPC_ADDRESS", Value: jmSvcName},
                        },
                        VolumeMounts: []corev1.VolumeMount{{
                            Name:      "flink-local-state",
                            MountPath: "/opt/flink/data",
                        }},
                    }},
                },
            },
            VolumeClaimTemplates: []corev1.PersistentVolumeClaim{{
                ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{Name: "flink-local-state"},
                Spec: corev1.PersistentVolumeClaimSpec{
                    AccessModes: []corev1.PersistentVolumeAccessMode{corev1.ReadWriteOnce},
                    Resources: corev1.VolumeResourceRequirements{
                        Requests: corev1.ResourceList{corev1.ResourceStorage: resource.MustParse("10Gi")},
                    },
                },
            }},
        },
    }

    if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
        return r.Create(ctx, desiredSTS)
    } else if err == nil {
        if *foundSTS.Spec.Replicas != replicas || foundSTS.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Image != image {
            foundSTS.Spec.Replicas = &replicas
            foundSTS.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Image = image
            return r.Update(ctx, foundSTS)
        }
    }
    return err
}

func (r *FlinkClusterReconciler) SetupWithManager(mgr ctrl.Manager) error {

### 📋 2. Execution Entry Point (`main.go`)

Ensure that the internal cluster scheme registrar loads OpenShift network routing API schemas explicitly alongside standard Core k8s client schemas before launching the manager engine loop.

```go
package main

import (
    "os"
    "strings"

    routev1 "://github.com"
    utilruntime "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/util/runtime"
    clientgoscheme "k8s.io/client-go/kubernetes/scheme"
    _ "k8s.io/client-go/plugin/pkg/client/auth"
    ctrl "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/cache"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/healthz"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/log/zap"
)

func init() {
    utilruntime.Must(clientgoscheme.AddToScheme(clientgoscheme.Scheme))
    // Explicit registration of OpenShift native routing objects
    utilruntime.Must(routev1.AddToScheme(clientgoscheme.Scheme))
}

func main() {
    ctrl.SetLogger(zap.New(zap.UseDevMode(false)))
    setupLog := ctrl.Log.WithName("setup")

    watchNamespacesEnv := os.Getenv("WATCH_NAMESPACES")
    var managerOptions ctrl.Options

    if watchNamespacesEnv != "" {
        namespaces := strings.Split(watchNamespacesEnv, ",")
        namespaceConfigs := make(map[string]cache.Config)
        for _, ns := range namespaces {
            namespaceConfigs[strings.TrimSpace(ns)] = cache.Config{}
        }
        managerOptions = ctrl.Options{
            Scheme: clientgoscheme.Scheme,
            Cache:  cache.Options{DefaultNamespaces: namespaceConfigs},
        }
    } else {
        managerOptions = ctrl.Options{Scheme: clientgoscheme.Scheme}
    }

    mgr, err := ctrl.NewManager(ctrl.GetConfigOrDie(), managerOptions)
    if err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "unable to initialize operator manager")
        os.Exit(1)
    }

    if err = (&FlinkClusterReconciler{
        Client: mgr.GetClient(),
        Scheme: mgr.GetScheme(),
    }).SetupWithManager(mgr); err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "unable to map reconciler parameters")
        os.Exit(1)
    }

    if err := mgr.AddHealthzCheck("healthz", healthz.Ping); err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "unable to mount network verification endpoints")
        os.Exit(1)
    }

    setupLog.Info("Starting Secure Flink 2.3 OpenShift Lifecycle Operator Bundle")
    if err := mgr.Start(ctrl.SetupSignalHandler()); err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "fatal core engine failure caught")
        os.Exit(1)
    }
}

googlesearch response

Here is the comprehensive, production-grade upgrade for your custom Flink 2.3 lifecycle operator.

This update integrates the last two requested features directly into the core reconciliation loop:

Network Isolation (NetworkPolicies): Restricts ingress traffic to the Flink cluster pods. It permits traffic only from the OpenShift Ingress Controller (via the Route), the operator itself, and internal cluster communication between JobManagers and TaskManagers.

🐹 1. Complete Production Controller (controller.go)

package main

import (
    "context"
    "fmt"
    "strconv"

    routev1 "://github.com"
    appsv1 "k8s.io/api/apps/v1"
    corev1 "k8s.io/api/core/v1"
    networkingv1 "k8s.io/api/networking/v1"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/errors"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/resource"
    metav1 "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/runtime"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/util/intstr"
    ctrl "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/client"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/log"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/predicate"
)

type FlinkClusterReconciler struct {
    client.Client
    Scheme *runtime.Scheme
}

func (r *FlinkClusterReconciler) Reconcile(ctx context.Context, req ctrl.Request) (ctrl.Result, error) {
    logger := log.FromContext(ctx)

    var configMap corev1.ConfigMap
    if err := r.Get(ctx, req.NamespacedName, &configMap); err != nil {
        if errors.IsNotFound(err) {
            logger.Info("Trigger ConfigMap deleted. Cascading deletion is handled via OwnerReferences.")
            return ctrl.Result{}, nil
        }
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    clusterMode := configMap.Data["cluster-mode"]
    image := configMap.Data["flink-image"]
    if image == "" {
        image = "flink:2.3"
    }
    parallelismStr := configMap.Data["parallelism"]
    parallelism, err := strconv.Atoi(parallelismStr)
    if err != nil || parallelism <= 0 {
        parallelism = 2
    }

    appJarPath := configMap.Data["app-jar-path"]
    entryClass := configMap.Data["job-entry-class"]
    savepointPath := configMap.Data["savepoint-path"]
    alertWebhookURL := configMap.Data["alert-webhook-url"] // FEATURE 2 Input

    // 1. Reconcile Network Security Layer (FEATURE 1)
    netPolicyName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-network-policy", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileNetworkPolicy(ctx, req.Namespace, netPolicyName, configMap.Name, &configMap); err != nil {
        logger.Error(err, "Failed to reconcile NetworkPolicy boundary", "Cluster", configMap.Name)
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    // 2. Reconcile Networking Layer Services
    jmServiceName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-jobmanager-rpc", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileService(ctx, req.Namespace, jmServiceName, configMap.Name, 6123, "rpc", &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    jmUIServiceName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-jobmanager-ui", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileService(ctx, req.Namespace, jmUIServiceName, configMap.Name, 8081, "ui", &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    // 3. Reconcile OpenShift Route for UI dashboard exposure
    jmUIServiceRouteName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-jobmanager-route", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileOpenShiftRoute(ctx, req.Namespace, jmUIServiceRouteName, jmUIServiceName, &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    // 4. Reconcile Infrastructure Deployments (with FEATURE 2 alert injection)
    jmDeploymentName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-jobmanager", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileJobManager(ctx, req.Namespace, jmDeploymentName, clusterMode, image, appJarPath, entryClass, savepointPath, alertWebhookURL, &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    tmStatefulSetName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-taskmanager", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileTaskManagers(ctx, req.Namespace, tmStatefulSetName, image, jmServiceName, int32(parallelism), &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    return ctrl.Result{}, nil
}

func (r *FlinkClusterReconciler) reconcileService(ctx context.Context, ns, name, clusterName string, port int32, portName string, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
    foundSVC := &corev1.Service{}
    err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: name}, foundSVC)
    if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
        svc := &corev1.Service{
            ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
                Name:      name,
                Namespace: ns,
                OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{
                    *metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind()),
                },
            },
            Spec: corev1.ServiceSpec{
                Selector: map[string]string{"flink-cluster": clusterName},
                Ports: []corev1.ServicePort{
                    {Port: port, Name: portName, TargetPort: intstr.FromInt32(port)},
                },
            },
        }
        return r.Create(ctx, svc)
    }
    return err
}

func (r *FlinkClusterReconciler) reconcileOpenShiftRoute(ctx context.Context, ns, name, svcName string, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
    foundRoute := &routev1.Route{}
    err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: name}, foundRoute)
    if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
        route := &routev1.Route{
            ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
                Name:      name,
                Namespace: ns,
                OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{
                    *metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind()),
                },
            },
            Spec: routev1.RouteSpec{
                To: routev1.RouteTargetReference{
                    Kind: "Service",
                    Name: svcName,
                },
                Port: &routev1.RoutePort{
                    TargetPort: intstr.FromString("ui"),
                },
                TLS: &routev1.TLSConfig{
                    Termination:                   routev1.TLSTerminationEdge,
                    InsecureEdgeTerminationPolicy: routev1.InsecureEdgeTerminationPolicyRedirect,
                },
            },
        }
        return r.Create(ctx, route)
    }
    return err
}

// FEATURE 1 Implementation: Programmatic NetworkPolicy Security Boundary
func (r *FlinkClusterReconciler) reconcileNetworkPolicy(ctx context.Context, ns, name, clusterName string, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
    foundNP := &networkingv1.NetworkPolicy{}
    err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: name}, foundNP)

    desiredNP := &networkingv1.NetworkPolicy{
        ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
            Name:      name,
            Namespace: ns,
            OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{
                *metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind()),
            },
        },
        Spec: networkingv1.NetworkPolicySpec{
            // Target all components matching this specific Flink Cluster label
            PodSelector: metav1.LabelSelector{
                MatchLabels: map[string]string{"flink-cluster": clusterName},
            },
            PolicyTypes: []networkingv1.PolicyType{networkingv1.PolicyTypeIngress},
            Ingress: []networkingv1.NetworkPolicyIngressRule{
                {
                    // Rule A: Allow intra-cluster pod communications (JobManager <-> TaskManager)
                    From: []networkingv1.NetworkPolicyPeer{
                        {
                            PodSelector: &metav1.LabelSelector{
                                MatchLabels: map[string]string{"flink-cluster": clusterName},
                            },
                        },
                    },
                },
                {
                    // Rule B: Allow external HTTP traffic coming strictly via OpenShift Router
                    From: []networkingv1.NetworkPolicyPeer{
                        {
                            NamespaceSelector: &metav1.LabelSelector{
                                MatchLabels: map[string]string{"network.openshift.io/policy-group": "ingress"},
                            },
                        },
                    },
                    Ports: []networkingv1.NetworkPolicyPort{
                        {
                            Port: &intstr.IntOrString{Type: intstr.Int, IntVal: 8081},
                        },
                    },
                },
            },
        },
    }

    if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
        return r.Create(ctx, desiredNP)
    }
    return err
}

func (r *FlinkClusterReconciler) reconcileJobManager(ctx context.Context, ns, name, mode, image, jarPath, entryClass, savepoint, webhookURL string, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
    foundJM := &appsv1.Deployment{}
    err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: name}, foundJM)

    var commandArgs []string
    if mode == "application" {
        commandArgs = []string{"standalone-job", fmt.Sprintf("--job-classname=%s", entryClass)}
        if savepoint != "" {
            commandArgs = append(commandArgs, fmt.Sprintf("--fromSavepoint=%s", savepoint))
        }
        if jarPath != "" {
            commandArgs = append(commandArgs, jarPath)
        }
    } else {
        commandArgs = []string{"jobmanager"}
    }

    // FEATURE 2 Implementation: Lifecycle hook wrapping with robust error handling script execution
    preStopCommand := "flink stop --stopWithSavepoint --savepointPath /opt/flink/data/savepoints $(flink list -r | grep RUNNING | awk '{print $4}')"
    if webhookURL != "" {
        // If the command fails, use curl to notify the external operations webhook
        preStopCommand = fmt.Sprintf("%s || curl -X POST -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{\"text\":\"CRITICAL: Flink PreStop Lifecycle savepoint dump failed for cluster %s\"}' %s", preStopCommand, owner.Name, webhookURL)
    } else {
        preStopCommand = fmt.Sprintf("%s || true", preStopCommand)
    }

    replicas := int32(1)
    desiredDeployment := &appsv1.Deployment{
        ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
            Name:      name,
            Namespace: ns,
            OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{
                *metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind()),
            },
        },
        Spec: appsv1.DeploymentSpec{
            Replicas: &replicas,
            Selector: &metav1.LabelSelector{MatchLabels: map[string]string{"flink-role": "jobmanager", "flink-cluster": owner.Name}},
            Template: corev1.PodTemplateSpec{
                Metadata: metav1.ObjectMeta{Labels: map[string]string{"flink-role": "jobmanager", "flink-cluster": owner.Name}},
                Spec: corev1.PodSpec{
                    Containers: []corev1.Container{{
                        Name:  "jobmanager",
                        Image: image,
                        Args:  commandArgs,
                        Ports: []corev1.ContainerPort{
                            {ContainerPort: 6123, Name: "rpc"},
                            {ContainerPort: 8081, Name: "ui"},
                        },
                        Env: []corev1.EnvVar{
                            {Name: "JOB_MANAGER_RPC_ADDRESS", Value: fmt.Sprintf("%s-jobmanager-rpc", owner.Name)},
                        },
                        Lifecycle: &corev1.Lifecycle{
                            PreStop: &corev1.LifecycleHandler{
                                Exec: &corev1.ExecAction{
                                    Command: []string{"/bin/sh", "-c", preStopCommand},
                                },
                            },
                        },
                        VolumeMounts: []corev1.VolumeMount{{
                            Name:      "flink-shared-storage",
                            MountPath: "/opt/flink/data",
                        }},
                    }},
                    Volumes: []corev1.Volume{{
                        Name: "flink-shared-storage",
                        VolumeSource: corev1.VolumeSource{
                            EmptyDir: &corev1.EmptyDirVolumeSource{},
                        },
                    }},
                },
            },
        },
    }

    if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
        return r.Create(ctx, desiredDeployment)
    } else if err == nil {
        if foundJM.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Image != image {
            foundJM.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Image = image

Use code with caution.

foundJM.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Args = commandArgs
return r.Update(ctx, foundJM)
}
}
return err
}

func (r *FlinkClusterReconciler) reconcileTaskManagers(ctx context.Context, ns, name, image, jmSvcName string, replicas int32, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
foundSTS := &appsv1.StatefulSet{}
err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: name}, foundSTS)

desiredSTS := &appsv1.StatefulSet{
ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
Name: name,
Namespace: ns,
OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{
*metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind()),
},
},
Spec: appsv1.StatefulSetSpec{
Replicas: &replicas,
ServiceName: jmSvcName,
PodManagementPolicy: appsv1.ParallelPodManagement,
Selector: &metav1.LabelSelector{
MatchLabels: map[string]string{"flink-role": "taskmanager", "flink-cluster": owner.Name},
},
Template: corev1.PodTemplateSpec{
Metadata: metav1.ObjectMeta{Labels: map[string]string{"flink-role": "taskmanager", "flink-cluster": owner.Name}},
Spec: corev1.PodSpec{
Containers: []corev1.Container{{
Name: "taskmanager",
Image: image,
Args: []string{"taskmanager"},
Env: []corev1.EnvVar{
{Name: "JOB_MANAGER_RPC_ADDRESS", Value: jmSvcName},
},
VolumeMounts: []corev1.VolumeMount{{
Name: "flink-local-state",
MountPath: "/opt/flink/data",
}},
}},
},
},
VolumeClaimTemplates: []corev1.PersistentVolumeClaim{{
ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{Name: "flink-local-state"},
Spec: corev1.PersistentVolumeClaimSpec{
AccessModes: []corev1.PersistentVolumeAccessMode{corev1.ReadWriteOnce},
Resources: corev1.VolumeResourceRequirements{
Requests: corev1.ResourceList{corev1.ResourceStorage: resource.MustParse("10Gi")},
},
},
}},
},
}

if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
return r.Create(ctx, desiredSTS)
} else if err == nil {
if *foundSTS.Spec.Replicas != replicas || foundSTS.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Image != image {
foundSTS.Spec.Replicas = &replicas
foundSTS.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Image = image
return r.Update(ctx, foundSTS)
}
}
return err
}

},

}

WithEventFilter(labelPredicate).

Owns(&appsv1.StatefulSet{}).
Owns(&corev1.Service{}).

Owns(&networkingv1.NetworkPolicy{}). // Track security asset drift state changes
Complete(r)

    setupLog.Info("Starting Hardened Multi-Namespace Flink 2.3 Operator Lifecycle Environment")
    if err := mgr.Start(ctrl.SetupSignalHandler()); err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "fatal core engine failure caught")
        os.Exit(1)
    }
}

More


you asked

message time: 2026-09-16 00:00:00

no ha active active active passive routeaways all fully automated full code prod ready


googlesearch response

To ensure high availability (HA) without complex active-active or active-passive data synchronization architectures, Flink relies on a centralized Active/Standby coordinator topology with automated leader election.

In this production-ready setup, multiple JobManager pods run concurrently within your namespace. A single instance is elected as the Active Leader, while the others remain as Passive Standbys. If the active node crashes, a standby is immediately promoted by the cluster, and the TaskManagers automatically reroute their internal data pipelines to the new leader without dropping stream state.

To automate this seamlessly inside an OpenShift namespace without high-privilege cluster roles, this code implements Kubernetes-native Leader Election using ConfigMaps/Leases for coordination, paired with an OpenShift Route that automatically targets only the Active leader node via label-switching.


🐹 1. Complete Production HA Lifecycle Controller (controller.go)

This updated code modifies the reconciliation loop to deploy multiple JobManager replicas configured with native Kubernetes coordination properties. It also provisions an optimized headless service infrastructure that enables immediate leader failovers.

package main

import (
    "context"
    "fmt"
    "strconv"

    routev1 "://github.com"
    appsv1 "k8s.io/api/apps/v1"
    corev1 "k8s.io/api/core/v1"
    networkingv1 "k8s.io/api/networking/v1"
    rbacv1 "k8s.io/api/rbac/v1"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/errors"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/resource"
    metav1 "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/runtime"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/util/intstr"
    ctrl "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/client"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/log"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/predicate"
)

type FlinkHAClusterReconciler struct {
    client.Client
    Scheme *runtime.Scheme
}

func (r *FlinkHAClusterReconciler) Reconcile(ctx context.Context, req ctrl.Request) (ctrl.Result, error) {
    logger := log.FromContext(ctx)

    var configMap corev1.ConfigMap
    if err := r.Get(ctx, req.NamespacedName, &configMap); err != nil {
        if errors.IsNotFound(err) {
            logger.Info("Trigger ConfigMap deleted. Cleanups handled via OwnerReferences.")
            return ctrl.Result{}, nil
        }
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    clusterMode := configMap.Data["cluster-mode"]
    image := configMap.Data["flink-image"]
    if image == "" {
        image = "flink:2.3"
    }
    parallelismStr := configMap.Data["parallelism"]
    parallelism, err := strconv.Atoi(parallelismStr)
    if err != nil || parallelism <= 0 {
        parallelism = 3
    }

    appJarPath := configMap.Data["app-jar-path"]
    entryClass := configMap.Data["job-entry-class"]
    savepointPath := configMap.Data["savepoint-path"]
    alertWebhookURL := configMap.Data["alert-webhook-url"]

    // 1. Reconcile Network Isolation Boundaries
    netPolicyName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-ha-policy", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileNetworkPolicy(ctx, req.Namespace, netPolicyName, configMap.Name, &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    // 2. AUTOMATION: Local Localized RBAC for Flink native leader election mechanism
    saName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-flink-ha-sa", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileLocalHAAuth(ctx, req.Namespace, saName, &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    // 3. Reconcile Active/Standby Services
    jmServiceName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-jobmanager-rpc", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileService(ctx, req.Namespace, jmServiceName, configMap.Name, 6123, "rpc", true, &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    jmUIServiceName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-jobmanager-ui", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileService(ctx, req.Namespace, jmUIServiceName, configMap.Name, 8081, "ui", false, &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    // 4. AUTOMATION: OpenShift Route targeting the active node via leader failover patterns
    jmUIServiceRouteName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-jobmanager-route", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileOpenShiftRoute(ctx, req.Namespace, jmUIServiceRouteName, jmUIServiceName, &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    // 5. Reconcile Multi-Replica (Active/Standby) HA JobManagers Deployment
    jmDeploymentName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-jobmanager", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileJobManagerHA(ctx, req.Namespace, jmDeploymentName, clusterMode, image, saName, appJarPath, entryClass, savepointPath, alertWebhookURL, &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    // 6. Reconcile TaskManager StatefulSet
    tmStatefulSetName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-taskmanager", configMap.Name)
    if err := r.reconcileTaskManagers(ctx, req.Namespace, tmStatefulSetName, image, jmServiceName, int32(parallelism), saName, &configMap); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    return ctrl.Result{}, nil
}

func (r *FlinkHAClusterReconciler) reconcileService(ctx context.Context, ns, name, clusterName string, port int32, portName string, isHeadless bool, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
    foundSVC := &corev1.Service{}
    err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: name}, foundSVC)
    if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
        svc := &corev1.Service{
            ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
                Name:      name,
                Namespace: ns,
                OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{*metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind())},
            },
            Spec: corev1.ServiceSpec{
                Selector: map[string]string{"flink-cluster": clusterName, "flink-role": "jobmanager"},
                Ports: []corev1.ServicePort{
                    {Port: port, Name: portName, TargetPort: intstr.FromInt32(port)},
                },
            },
        }
        if isHeadless {
            svc.Spec.ClusterIP = "None" // Core requirement for direct zookeeper-less actor routing
        }
        return r.Create(ctx, svc)
    }
    return err
}

func (r *FlinkHAClusterReconciler) reconcileOpenShiftRoute(ctx context.Context, ns, name, svcName string, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
    foundRoute := &routev1.Route{}
    err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: name}, foundRoute)
    if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
        route := &routev1.Route{
            ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
                Name:      name,
                Namespace: ns,
                OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{*metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind())},
            },
            Spec: routev1.RouteSpec{
                To:   routev1.RouteTargetReference{Kind: "Service", Name: svcName},
                Port: &routev1.RoutePort{TargetPort: intstr.FromString("ui")},
                TLS: &routev1.TLSConfig{
                    Termination:                   routev1.TLSTerminationEdge,
                    InsecureEdgeTerminationPolicy: routev1.InsecureEdgeTerminationPolicyRedirect,
                },
            },
        }
        return r.Create(ctx, route)
    }
    return err
}

// AUTOMATION: Configures low-privilege namespace RBAC dynamically so Flink can lock coordinates without cluster-admin roles
func (r *FlinkHAClusterReconciler) reconcileLocalHAAuth(ctx context.Context, ns, saName string, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
    foundSA := &corev1.ServiceAccount{}
    if err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: saName}, foundSA); err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
        sa := &corev1.ServiceAccount{
            ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{Name: saName, Namespace: ns, OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{*metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind())}},
        }
        if err := r.Create(ctx, sa); err != nil {
            return err
        }
    }

    roleName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-ha-leader-role", owner.Name)
    foundRole := &rbacv1.Role{}
    if err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: roleName}, foundRole); err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
        role := &rbacv1.Role{
            ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{Name: roleName, Namespace: ns, OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{*metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind())}},
            Rules: []rbacv1.PolicyRule{
                // ConfigMaps and Leases are used by Flink 2.3 for cluster consensus coordination tracking
                {APIGroups: []string{"", "coordination.k8s.io"}, Resources: []string{"configmaps", "leases"}, Verbs: []string{"get", "list", "watch", "create", "update", "patch", "delete"}},
                {APIGroups: []string{""}, Resources: []string{"pods", "services"}, Verbs: []string{"get", "list", "watch"}},
            },
        }
        if err := r.Create(ctx, role); err != nil {
            return err
        }
    }

    rbName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-ha-leader-rb", owner.Name)
    foundRB := &rbacv1.RoleBinding{}
    if err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: rbName}, foundRB); err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
        rb := &rbacv1.RoleBinding{
            ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{Name: rbName, Namespace: ns, OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{*metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind())}},
            Subjects:   []rbacv1.Subject{{Kind: "ServiceAccount", Name: saName, Namespace: ns}},
            RoleRef:    rbacv1.RoleRef{Kind: "Role", Name: roleName, APIGroup: "rbac.authorization.k8s.io"},
        }
        return r.Create(ctx, rb)
    }
    return nil
}

func (r *FlinkHAClusterReconciler) reconcileJobManagerHA(ctx context.Context, ns, name, mode, image, saName, jarPath, entryClass, savepoint, webhookURL string, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
    foundJM := &appsv1.Deployment{}
    err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: name}, foundJM)

    var commandArgs []string
    if mode == "application" {
        commandArgs = []string{"standalone-job", fmt.Sprintf("--job-classname=%s", entryClass)}
        if savepoint != "" {
            commandArgs = append(commandArgs, fmt.Sprintf("--fromSavepoint=%s", savepoint))
        }
        if jarPath != "" {
            commandArgs = append(commandArgs, jarPath)
        }
    } else {
        commandArgs = []string{"jobmanager"}
    }

    preStopCommand := "flink stop --stopWithSavepoint --savepointPath /opt/flink/data/savepoints $(flink list -r | grep RUNNING | awk '{print $4}')"
    if webhookURL != "" {
        preStopCommand = fmt.Sprintf("%s || curl -X POST -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{\"text\":\"HA ALERT: PreStop recovery tracking extraction failure recorded on %s\"}' %s", preStopCommand, owner.Name, webhookURL)
    } else {
        preStopCommand = fmt.Sprintf("%s || true", preStopCommand)
    }

    // AUTOMATION: High Availability Setup running 3 concurrent instances (1 Active Leader, 2 Passive Standbys)
    haReplicas := int32(3)
    desiredDeployment := &appsv1.Deployment{
        ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
            Name:      name,
            Namespace: ns,
            OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{*metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind())},
        },
        Spec: appsv1.DeploymentSpec{
            Replicas: &haReplicas,
            Selector: &metav1.LabelSelector{MatchLabels: map[string]string{"flink-role": "jobmanager", "flink-cluster": owner.Name}},
            Template: corev1.PodTemplateSpec{
                Metadata: metav1.ObjectMeta{Labels: map[string]string{"flink-role": "jobmanager", "flink-cluster": owner.Name}},
                Spec: corev1.PodSpec{
                    ServiceAccountName: saName, // Mount authorization identity securely
                    Containers: []corev1.Container{{
                        Name:  "jobmanager",
                        Image: image,
                        Args:  commandArgs,
                        Ports: []corev1.ContainerPort{
                            {ContainerPort: 6123, Name: "rpc"},
                            {ContainerPort: 8081, Name: "ui"},
                        },
                        Env: []corev1.EnvVar{
                            {Name: "JOB_MANAGER_RPC_ADDRESS", Value: fmt.Sprintf("%s-jobmanager-rpc", owner.Name)},
                        },

Use code with caution.

// Native Flink 2.3 embedded high-availability runtime properties overrides
EnvFrom: []corev1.EnvFromSource{
{ConfigMapRef: &corev1.ConfigMapEnvSource{LocalObjectReference: corev1.LocalObjectReference{Name: owner.Name}}},
},
Lifecycle: &corev1.Lifecycle{
PreStop: &corev1.LifecycleHandler{Exec: &corev1.ExecAction{Command: []string{"/bin/sh", "-c", preStopCommand}}},
},
VolumeMounts: []corev1.VolumeMount{{Name: "flink-shared-storage", MountPath: "/opt/flink/data"}},
}},
Volumes: []corev1.Volume{{Name: "flink-shared-storage", VolumeSource: corev1.VolumeSource{EmptyDir: &corev1.EmptyDirVolumeSource{}}}},
},
},
},
}

if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
return r.Create(ctx, desiredDeployment)
} else if err == nil {
if foundJM.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Image != image {
foundJM.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Image = image
foundJM.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Args = commandArgs
return r.Update(ctx, foundJM)
}
}
return err
}

func (r *FlinkHAClusterReconciler) reconcileTaskManagers(ctx context.Context, ns, name, image, jmSvcName string, replicas int32, saName string, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
foundSTS := &appsv1.StatefulSet{}
err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: name}, foundSTS)

desiredSTS := &appsv1.StatefulSet{
ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
Name: name,
Namespace: ns,
OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{*metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind())},
},
Spec: appsv1.StatefulSetSpec{
Replicas: &replicas,
ServiceName: jmSvcName,
PodManagementPolicy: appsv1.ParallelPodManagement,
Selector: &metav1.LabelSelector{
MatchLabels: map[string]string{"flink-role": "taskmanager", "flink-cluster": owner.Name},
},
Template: corev1.PodTemplateSpec{
Metadata: metav1.ObjectMeta{Labels: map[string]string{"flink-role": "taskmanager", "flink-cluster": owner.Name}},
Spec: corev1.PodSpec{
ServiceAccountName: saName,
Containers: []corev1.Container{{
Name: "taskmanager",
Image: image,
Args: []string{"taskmanager"},
Env: []corev1.EnvVar{
{Name: "JOB_MANAGER_RPC_ADDRESS", Value: jmSvcName},
},
EnvFrom: []corev1.EnvFromSource{
{ConfigMapRef: &corev1.ConfigMapEnvSource{LocalObjectReference: corev1.LocalObjectReference{Name: owner.Name}}},
},
VolumeMounts: []corev1.VolumeMount{{Name: "flink-local-state", MountPath: "/opt/flink/data"}},
}},
},
},
VolumeClaimTemplates: []corev1.PersistentVolumeClaim{{
ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{Name: "flink-local-state"},
Spec: corev1.PersistentVolumeClaimSpec{
AccessModes: []corev1.PersistentVolumeAccessMode{corev1.ReadWriteOnce},
Resources: corev1.VolumeResourceRequirements{Requests: corev1.ResourceList{corev1.ResourceStorage: resource.MustParse("10Gi")}},
},
}},
},
}

if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
return r.Create(ctx, desiredSTS)
} else if err == nil {
if *foundSTS.Spec.Replicas != replicas || foundSTS.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Image != image {
foundSTS.Spec.Replicas = &replicas
foundSTS.Spec.Template.Spec.Containers.Image = image
return r.Update(ctx, foundSTS)
}
}
return err
}

func (r *FlinkHAClusterReconciler) reconcileNetworkPolicy(ctx context.Context, ns, name, clusterName string, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
foundNP := &networkingv1.NetworkPolicy{}
err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: name}, foundNP)

desiredNP := &networkingv1.NetworkPolicy{
ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
Name: name,
Namespace: ns,
OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{*metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind())},
},
Spec: networkingv1.NetworkPolicySpec{
PodSelector: metav1.LabelSelector{MatchLabels: map[string]string{"flink-cluster": clusterName}},
PolicyTypes: []networkingv1.PolicyType{networkingv1.PolicyTypeIngress},
Ingress: []networkingv1.NetworkPolicyIngressRule{
{
From: []networkingv1.NetworkPolicyPeer{{PodSelector: &metav1.LabelSelector{MatchLabels: map[string]string{"flink-cluster": clusterName}}}},
},
{
From: []networkingv1.NetworkPolicyPeer{{NamespaceSelector: &metav1.LabelSelector{MatchLabels: map[string]string{"network.openshift.io/policy-group": "ingress"}}}},
Ports: []networkingv1.NetworkPolicyPort{{Port: &intstr.IntOrString{Type: intstr.Int, IntVal: 8081}}},
},
},
},
}

if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
return r.Create(ctx, desiredNP)
}
return err
}

func (r *FlinkHAClusterReconciler) SetupWithManager(mgr ctrl.Manager) error {
labelPredicate, err := predicate.LabelSelectorPredicate(metav1.LabelSelector{
MatchLabels: map[string]string{"app.kubernetes.io/managed-by": "custom-flink-lifecycle-manager"},
})
if err != nil {
return err
}

return ctrl.NewControllerManagedBy(mgr).
For(&corev1.ConfigMap{}).
WithEventFilter(labelPredicate).
Owns(&appsv1.Deployment{}).
Owns(&appsv1.StatefulSet{}).
Owns(&corev1.Service{}).
Owns(&routev1.Route{}).
Owns(&networkingv1.NetworkPolicy{}).
Owns(&rbacv1.Role{}).
Owns(&rbacv1.RoleBinding{}).
Owns(&corev1.ServiceAccount{}).
Complete(r)
}


---

### 📋 2. Execution Entry Point (`main.go`)

```go
package main

import (
    "os"
    "strings"

    routev1 "://github.com"
    utilruntime "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/util/runtime"
    clientgoscheme "k8s.io/client-go/kubernetes/scheme"
    _ "k8s.io/client-go/plugin/pkg/client/auth"
    ctrl "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/cache"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/healthz"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/log/zap"
)

func init() {
    utilruntime.Must(clientgoscheme.AddToScheme(clientgoscheme.Scheme))
    utilruntime.Must(routev1.AddToScheme(clientgoscheme.Scheme))
}

func main() {
    ctrl.SetLogger(zap.New(zap.UseDevMode(false)))
    setupLog := ctrl.Log.WithName("setup")

    watchNamespacesEnv := os.Getenv("WATCH_NAMESPACES")
    var managerOptions ctrl.Options

    if watchNamespacesEnv != "" {
        namespaces := strings.Split(watchNamespacesEnv, ",")
        namespaceConfigs := make(map[string]cache.Config)
        for _, ns := range namespaces {
            namespaceConfigs[strings.TrimSpace(ns)] = cache.Config{}
        }
        managerOptions = ctrl.Options{
            Scheme: clientgoscheme.Scheme,
            Cache:  cache.Options{DefaultNamespaces: namespaceConfigs},
        }
    } else {
        managerOptions = ctrl.Options{Scheme: clientgoscheme.Scheme}
    }

    mgr, err := ctrl.NewManager(ctrl.GetConfigOrDie(), managerOptions)
    if err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "unable to initialize operator manager")
        os.Exit(1)
    }

    if err = (&FlinkHAClusterReconciler{
        Client: mgr.GetClient(),
        Scheme: mgr.GetScheme(),
    }).SetupWithManager(mgr); err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "unable to map metrics processing blocks")
        os.Exit(1)
    }

    if err := mgr.AddHealthzCheck("healthz", healthz.Ping); err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "unable to establish tracking health paths")
        os.Exit(1)
    }

    setupLog.Info("Starting Low-Privilege Flink 2.3 Automated Active/Standby HA Operator")
    if err := mgr.Start(ctrl.SetupSignalHandler()); err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "fatal operator runtime crash")
        os.Exit(1)
    }
}

FROM golang:1.22 AS builder
WORKDIR /workspace

COPY go.mod go.mod
COPY go.sum go.sum
RUN go mod download

FROM ://redhat.com

USER 65532:65532
ENTRYPOINT ["/manager"]

🏷 4. High-Availability Production Blueprint (ha-trigger.yaml)

Deploy this manifest to launch a resilient cluster. The flink-conf.yaml settings inject natively into Flink 2.3, causing the JobManager containers to automatically execute leader election loops inside your namespace boundary.

apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: dynamic-ha-session-cluster
  namespace: namespace-alpha
  labels:
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: custom-flink-lifecycle-manager
data:
  cluster-mode: "session"
  flink-image: "://docker.com"
  parallelism: "6"
  alert-webhook-url: "https://slack.com"

  # AUTOMATION: Native Flink 2.3 Kubernetes Zookeeper-less Leader Election Keys
  flink-conf.yaml: |
    high-availability.type: kubernetes
    high-availability.storageDir: s3://corporate-flink-checkpoints/ha-metadata/
    high-availability.cluster-id: dynamic-ha-session-cluster
    kubernetes.cluster-id: dynamic-ha-session-cluster

Use code with caution.

you asked

message time: 2026-09-16 00:00:00

shoukdnt i use helm? and shouldnt the operator use helm as well ?


googlesearch response

Using Helm directly vs. building a Custom Operator that leverages Helm internally is one of the most heavily debated architectural choices in cloud-native engineering.

The short answer is: You shouldn't use an imperative Go operator to manually code templates if you can use Helm, but you should use a Helm-backed Operator to manage the day-to-day lifecycle.

Here is the breakdown of why this shift makes sense, along with the production-ready full source code for a custom Go operator that uses Helm programmatically to manage your Flink 2.3 clusters.


⚖️ Why Use a Helm-Backed Operator?

Why not code raw YAML in Go? Hardcoding Kubernetes objects in Go (like we did in previous iterations) requires you to maintain massive structs. Every time you want to change a volume mount, add an environment variable, or tweak memory settings, you have to modify Go code, recompile, and rebuild your Docker image.
Why not just use Helm directly via CLI? While Helm is incredible for initial installation and upgrades (helm upgrade --install), it is not an operator. It cannot perform proactive maintenance. It cannot poll the Flink REST API, watch for failing jobs, orchestrate stateful savepoints before an upgrade, or dynamically react to active cluster failures.
The Hybrid Solution: You package your Flink cluster architecture using standard, flexible Helm charts hosted in Artifactory. Then, you write an operator whose sole job is to programmatically invoke the Helm SDK (helm/helm/v3). Your operator acts as a continuous reconciliation engine that applies your chart to your namespaces.


🐹 1. Full Production Helm-Driven Operator (controller.go)

This Go controller integrates the official Helm v3 Go SDK. Instead of defining deployments or stateful sets in Go, it downloads your Flink chart from Artifactory (or uses a bundled chart) and applies changes dynamically based on the trigger ConfigMap.

package main

import (
    "context"
    "fmt"
    "os"

    corev1 "k8s.io/api/core/v1"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/errors"
    metav1 "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/runtime"
    ctrl "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/client"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/log"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/predicate"

    // Native Helm v3 Go SDK packages
    "helm.sh/helm/v3/pkg/action"
    "helm.sh/helm/v3/pkg/chart/loader"
    "helm.sh/helm/v3/pkg/cli"
)

type FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler struct {
    client.Client
    Scheme *runtime.Scheme
}

var settings = cli.New()

func (r *FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler) Reconcile(ctx context.Context, req ctrl.Request) (ctrl.Result, error) {
    logger := log.FromContext(ctx)

    var configMap corev1.ConfigMap
    if err := r.Get(ctx, req.NamespacedName, &configMap); err != nil {
        if errors.IsNotFound(err) {
            // Trigger a proactive uninstallation if the ConfigMap is removed
            logger.Info("ConfigMap deleted. Uninstalling associated Helm cluster release.")
            return ctrl.Result{}, r.uninstallHelmRelease(req.Namespace, req.Name)
        }
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    // 1. Gather configuration details to map out Helm values overrides
    image := configMap.Data["flink-image"]
    parallelism := configMap.Data["parallelism"]
    clusterMode := configMap.Data["cluster-mode"]

    // Create a strongly-typed values structure map to feed to Helm
    helmValues := map[string]interface{}{
        "clusterMode": clusterMode,
        "image": map[string]interface{}{
            "repository": "://docker.com",
            "tag":        image,
        },
        "taskmanager": map[string]interface{}{
            "replicas": parallelism,
        },
    }

    // 2. Perform automated programmatic Helm Upgrade / Install execution loop
    releaseName := fmt.Sprintf("flink-ha-%s", configMap.Name)
    err := r.upgradeOrInstallHelmRelease(req.Namespace, releaseName, helmValues)
    if err != nil {
        logger.Error(err, "Failed to apply Helm release updates safely", "Release", releaseName)
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    logger.Info("Successfully completed Helm reconciliation routine", "Release", releaseName)
    return ctrl.Result{}, nil
}

func (r *FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler) upgradeOrInstallHelmRelease(namespace, releaseName string, values map[string]interface{}) error {
    // Initialize action configuration mapping into the local OpenShift storage driver namespace scope
    actionConfig := new(action.Configuration)
    if err := actionConfig.Init(settings.RESTClientGetter(), namespace, os.Getenv("HELM_DRIVER"), ctrl.Log.WithName("helm").Info); err != nil {
        return err
    }

    // Read chart files from local operator cache path (pulled or pre-bundled from Artifactory)
    chartPath := "/opt/charts/flink-cluster-2.3"
    chart, err := loader.Load(chartPath)
    if err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to load base Helm chart template: %w", err)
    }

    // Look up if a release tracking state already exists in the boundary
    histClient := action.NewHistory(actionConfig)
    histClient.Max = 1
    _, err = histClient.Run(releaseName)

    if err == action.ErrReleaseNotFound {
        // Imperative Fresh Deployment Execution Path
        installClient := action.NewInstall(actionConfig)
        installClient.ReleaseName = releaseName
        installClient.Namespace = namespace
        installClient.CreateNamespace = false
        _, err = installClient.Run(chart, values)
        return err
    } else if err != nil {
        return err
    }

    // Imperative Upgrade/Maintenance Rollout Execution Path
    upgradeClient := action.NewUpgrade(actionConfig)
    upgradeClient.Namespace = namespace
    _, err = upgradeClient.Run(releaseName, chart, values)
    return err
}

func (r *FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler) uninstallHelmRelease(namespace, releaseName string) error {
    actionConfig := new(action.Configuration)
    if err := actionConfig.Init(settings.RESTClientGetter(), namespace, os.Getenv("HELM_DRIVER"), ctrl.Log.WithName("helm").Info); err != nil {
        return err
    }

    uninstallClient := action.NewUninstall(actionConfig)
    _, err := uninstallClient.Run(fmt.Sprintf("flink-ha-%s", releaseName))
    if err == action.ErrReleaseNotFound {
        return nil // Graceful exit if it was already deleted
    }
    return err
}

func (r *FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler) SetupWithManager(mgr ctrl.Manager) error {
    labelPredicate, err := predicate.LabelSelectorPredicate(metav1.LabelSelector{
        MatchLabels: map[string]string{"app.kubernetes.io/managed-by": "custom-flink-helm-operator"},
    })
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }

    return ctrl.NewControllerManagedBy(mgr).
        For(&corev1.ConfigMap{}).
        WithEventFilter(labelPredicate).
        Complete(r)
}

Use code with caution.


📋 2. Execution Entry Point (main.go)

package main

import (
    "os"
    "strings"

    clientgoscheme "k8s.io/client-go/kubernetes/scheme"
    _ "k8s.io/client-go/plugin/pkg/client/auth"
    ctrl "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/cache"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/healthz"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/log/zap"
)

func main() {
    ctrl.SetLogger(zap.New(zap.UseDevMode(false)))
    setupLog := ctrl.Log.WithName("setup")

    watchNamespacesEnv := os.Getenv("WATCH_NAMESPACES")
    var managerOptions ctrl.Options

    if watchNamespacesEnv != "" {
        namespaces := strings.Split(watchNamespacesEnv, ",")
        namespaceConfigs := make(map[string]cache.Config)
        for _, ns := range namespaces {
            namespaceConfigs[strings.TrimSpace(ns)] = cache.Config{}
        }
        managerOptions = ctrl.Options{
            Scheme: clientgoscheme.Scheme,
            Cache:  cache.Options{DefaultNamespaces: namespaceConfigs},
        }
    } else {
        managerOptions = ctrl.Options{Scheme: clientgoscheme.Scheme}
    }

    mgr, err := ctrl.NewManager(ctrl.GetConfigOrDie(), managerOptions)
    if err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "unable to start operator engine manager context")
        os.Exit(1)
    }

    if err = (&FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler{
        Client: mgr.GetClient(),
        Scheme: mgr.GetScheme(),
    }).SetupWithManager(mgr); err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "unable to map helm deployment logic pipelines")
        os.Exit(1)
    }

    if err := mgr.AddHealthzCheck("healthz", healthz.Ping); err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "unable to mount network lifecycle paths")
        os.Exit(1)
    }

    setupLog.Info("Starting Custom Helm-SDK Powered Flink 2.3 Controller Engine")
    if err := mgr.Start(ctrl.SetupSignalHandler()); err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "fatal core processing system failure encountered")
        os.Exit(1)
    }
}

Use code with caution.


📦 3. Corporate Multi-Stage Hardened Dockerfile (Dockerfile)

This Dockerfile downloads your stable, production-tested Flink Helm Chart straight from your Artifactory proxy during compilation and bundles it natively inside the final scratch container.

# Stage 1: Fetch components and compile Go binary
FROM golang:1.22 AS builder
WORKDIR /workspace

# Install system dependencies needed to communicate over TLS with Artifactory
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y curl tar

# Pull the production Helm chart template tarball from Artifactory
RUN mkdir -p /workspace/charts && \
    curl -L -u "user:password" "https://yourcorp.com" | tar -xz -C /workspace/charts

COPY go.mod go.mod
COPY go.sum go.sum
RUN go mod download

COPY main.go main.go
COPY controller.go controller.go

RUN CGO_ENABLED=0 GOOS=linux GOARCH=amd64 go build -a -o manager main.go controller.go

# Stage 2: Construct the lightweight runtime image
FROM ://redhat.com
WORKDIR /

COPY --from=builder /workspace/manager .
COPY --from=builder /workspace/charts/flink-cluster-2.3 /opt/charts/flink-cluster-2.3

# Set Helm metadata driver environment variables to use standard K8s secrets/configmaps
ENV HELM_DRIVER=secret

USER 65532:65532
ENTRYPOINT ["/manager"]

message time: 2026-09-16 00:00:00

do 3 prod ready full code also naybe the operator use tbe kub api for some things which havent been implemented in helm charts?


googlesearch response

Combining the programmatic Helm v3 SDK with direct Kubernetes API calls is the ultimate enterprise operator pattern. It lets Helm handle 90% of the core resource generation, while the Go operator uses the direct K8s API to intercept, validate, and inject properties not natively exposed in the charts.

This complete, production-grade upgrade implements your requested Automated Rollback Workflow, a Pre-Upgrade Security Validation Engine, and Fallback K8s API Operations for items missing from the Helm templates (such as dynamic Namespace-scoped RBAC roles and customized deployment validation checks).


🐹 1. Complete Production Controller (controller.go)

This controller performs a pre-upgrade baseline check, applies the Helm release, runs a live watch on the newly rolled-out pods, and programmatically issues an immediate Rollback using the Helm SDK if the new deployment fails to reach a healthy status. It also falls back to direct Kubernetes API manipulation to apply low-privilege RBAC roles not supported inside the Artifactory chart templates.

package main

import (
    "context"
    "fmt"
    "os"
    "strconv"
    "time"

    appsv1 "k8s.io/api/apps/v1"
    corev1 "k8s.io/api/core/v1"
    rbacv1 "k8s.io/api/rbac/v1"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/errors"
    metav1 "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1"
    "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/runtime"
    ctrl "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/client"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/log"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/predicate"

    // Native Helm v3 Go SDK packages
    "helm.sh/helm/v3/pkg/action"
    "helm.sh/helm/v3/pkg/chart/loader"
    "helm.sh/helm/v3/pkg/cli"
)

type FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler struct {
    client.Client
    Scheme *runtime.Scheme
}

var settings = cli.New()

func (r *FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler) Reconcile(ctx context.Context, req ctrl.Request) (ctrl.Result, error) {
    logger := log.FromContext(ctx)

    var configMap corev1.ConfigMap
    if err := r.Get(ctx, req.NamespacedName, &configMap); err != nil {
        if errors.IsNotFound(err) {
            logger.Info("ConfigMap deleted. Reverting and uninstalling Helm release.")
            return ctrl.Result{}, r.uninstallHelmRelease(req.Namespace, req.Name)
        }
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    // FEATURE 2: Pre-Upgrade Validation Engine (Check against security baselines)
    if err := r.validateSecurityBaselines(&configMap); err != nil {
        logger.Error(err, "SECURITY REJECTION: Trigger ConfigMap values violate container standards. Halting deployment.")
        return ctrl.Result{}, nil // Halt execution without crashing the operator
    }

    // EXTENSION: Direct K8s API Operations for items missing in the Helm chart
    // Dynamically configure low-privilege namespace RBAC roles required by the Flink cluster
    if err := r.ensureDirectK8sRBAC(ctx, req.Namespace, configMap.Name, &configMap); err != nil {
        logger.Error(err, "Failed to apply dynamic namespace RBAC roles via fallback K8s API calls")
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    // Gather value mappings for the Helm SDK engine
    image := configMap.Data["flink-image"]
    parallelism := configMap.Data["parallelism"]
    clusterMode := configMap.Data["cluster-mode"]

    helmValues := map[string]interface{}{
        "clusterMode": clusterMode,
        "image": map[string]interface{}{
            "repository": "://docker.com",
            "tag":        image,
        },
        "taskmanager": map[string]interface{}{
            "replicas": parallelism,
        },
    }

    releaseName := fmt.Sprintf("flink-ha-%s", configMap.Name)

    // Perform the lifecycle installation or upgrade
    err := r.upgradeOrInstallHelmRelease(req.Namespace, releaseName, helmValues)
    if err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, err
    }

    // FEATURE 3: Automated Rollback Workflow
    // Verify that the freshly applied deployment reaches a healthy state. If it times out or fails, roll back to the previous version.
    logger.Info("Evaluating post-upgrade health benchmarks for the new release...", "Release", releaseName)
    go r.monitorAndRollbackOnFailure(req.Namespace, releaseName, configMap.Name)

    return ctrl.Result{}, nil
}

// FEATURE 2: Security Validation Logic
func (r *FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler) validateSecurityBaselines(cm *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
    parallelismStr := cm.Data["parallelism"]
    if parallelismStr != "" {
        p, err := strconv.Atoi(parallelismStr)
        if err == nil && p > 32 {
            return fmt.Errorf("parallelism threshold '%d' exceeds maximum multi-tenant namespace boundary quota limit of 32", p)
        }
    }

    // Ban root-user target configurations inside the values block if attempted
    if cm.Data["allow-privileged-execution"] == "true" {
        return fmt.Errorf("prohibited configuration change: 'allow-privileged-execution=true' violates corporate baseline security profiles")
    }
    return nil
}

// EXTENSION: Fallback Direct K8s API Calls for configurations missing from the Helm chart
func (r *FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler) ensureDirectK8sRBAC(ctx context.Context, ns, clusterName string, owner *corev1.ConfigMap) error {
    roleName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-namespace-accessor", clusterName)
    foundRole := &rbacv1.Role{}

    err := r.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKey{Namespace: ns, Name: roleName}, foundRole)
    if err != nil && errors.IsNotFound(err) {
        desiredRole := &rbacv1.Role{
            ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
                Name:      roleName,
                Namespace: ns,
                OwnerReferences: []metav1.OwnerReference{
                    *metav1.NewControllerRef(owner, owner.GroupVersionKind()),
                },
            },
            Rules: []rbacv1.PolicyRule{
                {
                    APIGroups: []string{""},
                    Resources: []string{"configmaps", "pods"},
                    Verbs:     []string{"get", "list", "watch", "update", "patch"},
                },
            },
        }
        return r.Create(ctx, desiredRole)
    }
    return err
}

func (r *FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler) upgradeOrInstallHelmRelease(namespace, releaseName string, values map[string]interface{}) error {
    actionConfig := new(action.Configuration)
    if err := actionConfig.Init(settings.RESTClientGetter(), namespace, os.Getenv("HELM_DRIVER"), ctrl.Log.WithName("helm").Info); err != nil {
        return err
    }

    chartPath := "/opt/charts/flink-cluster-2.3"
    chart, err := loader.Load(chartPath)
    if err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to load embedded chart structure: %w", err)
    }

    histClient := action.NewHistory(actionConfig)
    histClient.Max = 1
    _, err = histClient.Run(releaseName)

    if err == action.ErrReleaseNotFound {
        installClient := action.NewInstall(actionConfig)
        installClient.ReleaseName = releaseName
        installClient.Namespace = namespace
        installClient.CreateNamespace = false
        _, err = installClient.Run(chart, values)
        return err
    } else if err != nil {
        return err
    }

    upgradeClient := action.NewUpgrade(actionConfig)
    upgradeClient.Namespace = namespace
    _, err = upgradeClient.Run(releaseName, chart, values)
    return err
}

// FEATURE 3: Automated Rollback Asynchronous Routine
func (r *FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler) monitorAndRollbackOnFailure(namespace, releaseName, clusterName string) {
    ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 3*time.Minute)
    defer cancel()

    ticker := time.NewTicker(10 * time.Second)
    defer ticker.Stop()

    jmDeploymentName := fmt.Sprintf("flink-ha-%s-jobmanager", clusterName)

    for {
        select {
        case <-ctx.Done():
            // The update timed out before reaching a healthy state. Initiate an immediate rollback.
            ctrl.Log.WithName("rollback-engine").Error(nil, "TIMEOUT ENCOUNTERED: New deployment failed to become ready within 3 minutes. Executing automated Helm rollback.", "Release", releaseName)
            _ = r.executeHelmRollback(namespace, releaseName)
            return

        case <-ticker.C:
            var jmDep appsv1.Deployment
            err := r.Get(context.Background(), client.ObjectKey{Namespace: namespace, Name: jmDeploymentName}, &jmDep)
            if err == nil {
                // Check if the replicas are fully initialized and healthy
                if jmDep.Status.ReadyReplicas == jmDep.Status.Replicas && jmDep.Status.Replicas > 0 {
                    ctrl.Log.WithName("rollback-engine").Info("Deployment validation passed. Cluster rollout is healthy.", "Release", releaseName)
                    return
                }

                // Handle crash loops immediately without waiting for the full timeout
                for _, cond := range jmDep.Status.Conditions {
                    if cond.Type == appsv1.DeploymentReplicaFailure && cond.Status == corev1.ConditionTrue {
                        ctrl.Log.WithName("rollback-engine").Error(nil, "CRASH LOOP DETECTED: Pod provisioning has failed. Initiating automated Helm rollback immediately.", "Release", releaseName)
                        _ = r.executeHelmRollback(namespace, releaseName)
                        return
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

func (r *FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler) executeHelmRollback(namespace, releaseName string) error {
    actionConfig := new(action.Configuration)
    if err := actionConfig.Init(settings.RESTClientGetter(), namespace, os.Getenv("HELM_DRIVER"), ctrl.Log.WithName("helm").Info); err != nil {
        return err
    }

    rollbackClient := action.NewRollback(actionConfig)
    rollbackClient.Version = 0 // Setting to 0 programmatically falls back exactly to the previous working revision
    rollbackClient.CleanupOnFail = true

    return rollbackClient.Run(releaseName)
}

func (r *FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler) uninstallHelmRelease(namespace, releaseName string) error {
    actionConfig := new(action.Configuration)
    if err := actionConfig.Init(settings.RESTClientGetter(), namespace, os.Getenv("HELM_DRIVER"), ctrl.Log.WithName("helm").Info); err != nil {
        return err
    }
    uninstallClient := action.NewUninstall(actionConfig)
    _, err := uninstallClient.Run(fmt.Sprintf("flink-ha-%s", releaseName))
    if err == action.ErrReleaseNotFound {
        return nil
    }
    return err
}

func (r *FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler) SetupWithManager(mgr ctrl.Manager) error {
    labelPredicate, err := predicate.LabelSelectorPredicate(metav1.LabelSelector{
        MatchLabels: map[string]string{"app.kubernetes.io/managed-by": "custom-flink-helm-operator"},
    })
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }

    return ctrl.NewControllerManagedBy(mgr).
        For(&corev1.ConfigMap{}).
        WithEventFilter(labelPredicate).
        Owns(&rbacv1.Role{}). // Track direct K8s API components for state mutations
        Complete(r)
}

Use code with caution.


📋 2. Execution Entry Point (main.go)

package main

import (
    "os"
    "strings"

    clientgoscheme "k8s.io/client-go/kubernetes/scheme"
    _ "k8s.io/client-go/plugin/pkg/client/auth"
    ctrl "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/cache"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/healthz"
    "sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/log/zap"
)

func main() {
    ctrl.SetLogger(zap.New(zap.UseDevMode(false)))
    setupLog := ctrl.Log.WithName("setup")

    watchNamespacesEnv := os.Getenv("WATCH_NAMESPACES")
    var managerOptions ctrl.Options

    if watchNamespacesEnv != "" {
        namespaces := strings.Split(watchNamespacesEnv, ",")
        namespaceConfigs := make(map[string]cache.Config)
        for _, ns := range namespaces {
            namespaceConfigs[strings.TrimSpace(ns)] = cache.Config{}
        }
        managerOptions = ctrl.Options{
            Scheme: clientgoscheme.Scheme,
            Cache:  cache.Options{DefaultNamespaces: namespaceConfigs},
        }
    } else {
        managerOptions = ctrl.Options{Scheme: clientgoscheme.Scheme}
    }

    mgr, err := ctrl.NewManager(ctrl.GetConfigOrDie(), managerOptions)
    if err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "unable to start operator context manager")
        os.Exit(1)
    }

    if err = (&FlinkHelmOperatorReconciler{
        Client: mgr.GetClient(),
        Scheme: mgr.GetScheme(),
    }).SetupWithManager(mgr); err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "unable to mount advanced helm-backed pipeline controller")
        os.Exit(1)
    }

    if err := mgr.AddHealthzCheck("healthz", healthz.Ping); err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "unable to mount network telemetry indicators")
        os.Exit(1)
    }

    setupLog.Info("Starting Hardened Helm + Core K8s API Multi-Capability Flink Controller Engine")
    if err := mgr.Start(ctrl.SetupSignalHandler()); err != nil {
        setupLog.Error(err, "fatal core tracking processor loop error encountered")
        os.Exit(1)
    }
}

📋 Feature 1: Operator Deployment and Low-Privilege RBAC Manifests (operator-deployment.yaml)

Deploy these manifests to run the operator itself in your cluster. This matches the exact baseline architecture defined across your design constraints.

apiVersion: v1
kind: ServiceAccount
metadata:
  name: flink-helm-operator-sa
  namespace: your-operator-namespace
---
apiVersion: rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1
kind: Role
metadata:
  name: flink-helm-operator-role
  namespace: target-workspace-namespace
rules:
  # Permissions to manage trigger ConfigMaps
  - apiGroups: [""]
    resources: ["configmaps"]
    verbs: ["get", "list", "watch", "update", "patch"]
  # Helm SDK metadata tracking permissions
  - apiGroups: [""]
    resources: ["secrets"]
    verbs: ["get", "list", "watch", "create", "update", "patch", "delete"]
  # Core cluster resource lifecycle management permissions (Helm outputs)
  - apiGroups: ["apps", ""]
    resources: ["deployments", "statefulsets", "pods", "services"]
    verbs: ["get", "list", "watch", "create", "update", "patch", "delete"]
  # Fallback Direct K8s API configuration permissions (Missing in Helm)
  - apiGroups: ["rbac.authorization.k8s.io"]
    resources: ["roles"]
    verbs: ["get", "list", "watch", "create", "update", "patch"]
---
apiVersion: rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1
kind: RoleBinding
metadata:
  name: flink-helm-operator-rb
  namespace: target-workspace-namespace
subjects:
  - kind: ServiceAccount
    name: flink-helm-operator-sa
    namespace: your-operator-namespace
roleRef:
  kind: Role
  name: flink-helm-operator-role
  apiGroup: rbac.authorization.k8s.io
---
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: flink-helm-operator
  namespace: your-operator-namespace
spec:
  replicas: 1
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      control-plane: flink-helm-controller
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        control-plane: flink-helm-controller
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: flink-helm-operator-sa
      containers:
        - name: operator
          image: image-registry.openshift-image-registry.svc:5000/your-operator-namespace/flink-helm-operator:latest
          imagePullPolicy: Always
          env:
            - name: WATCH_NAMESPACES
              value: "target-workspace-namespace"
          resources:
            limits:
              cpu: "500m"
              memory: "512Mi"
            requests:
              cpu: "100m"
              memory: "128Mi"

Use code with caution.
